function currentLanguage(){try{const raw=localStorage.getItem('tlg_language');const lang=raw?JSON.parse(raw):'en';return lang==='ja'?'ja':'en';}catch(e){return 'en';}}
function uiText(key){const dict=currentLanguage()==='ja'?JP_UI:EN_UI;return dict[key]||EN_UI[key]||key;}
function setLanguage(lang){localStorage.setItem('tlg_language',JSON.stringify(lang==='ja'?'ja':'en'));applyLanguage();if(route&&scene){document.getElementById('stat1name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA[route].meta[0]);document.getElementById('stat2name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA[route].meta[1]);render();}else{renderRoutes();}}
function applyLanguage(){const lang=currentLanguage();document.documentElement.lang=lang;document.body.classList.toggle('jp-font',lang==='ja');document.title=uiText('pageTitle');const en=document.getElementById('langCardEn'),ja=document.getElementById('langCardJa');if(en)en.classList.toggle('active',lang==='en');if(ja)ja.classList.toggle('active',lang==='ja');const map={settingsTitle:'settingsTitle',settingsIntro:'settingsIntro',settingsNote:'settingsNote'};Object.entries(map).forEach(([id,key])=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=uiText(key);});document.querySelectorAll('#advanceRow button').forEach(b=>b.textContent=uiText('next'));document.querySelectorAll('.status-row .stat b').forEach(b=>{b.textContent=localizeStatLabel(b.textContent);});}
function localizeNameToken(name){if(currentLanguage()!=='ja')return name==='Airi'?playerName():name;if(name==='Airi')return playerName()==='Airi'?'アイリ':playerName();return JP_SPEAKERS[name]||name;}
function localizeSpeaker(speaker){return localizeNameToken(String(speaker||''));}
function localizeStatLabel(label){return currentLanguage()==='ja'?(JP_STAT_LABELS[label]||label):label;}
function localizeRouteName(r,locked=false){if(currentLanguage()==='ja')return locked?JP_UI.lockedRoute:(r==='kenji'?'佐藤ケンジ':(JP_ROUTE_NAMES[r]||routeNames[r]));return locked?EN_UI.lockedRoute:(r==='kenji'?'Kenji Sato':routeNames[r]);}
function localizeRouteTag(r){return currentLanguage()==='ja'?(JP_ROUTE_TAGS[r]||routeTags[r]):routeTags[r];}
function localizeSceneTitle(sceneKey,fallback){return currentLanguage()==='ja'?(JP_SCENE_TITLES[sceneKey]||fallback):fallback;}
function localizeStoryText(t){let raw=String(t||'');const lang=currentLanguage();if(lang!=='ja')return raw.replaceAll('Airi',playerName());if(scene&&JP_STORY[scene]&&raw===((DATA[route]&&DATA[route].scenes[scene]&&DATA[route].scenes[scene][2])||'')){return JP_STORY[scene].replaceAll('アイリ',localizeNameToken('Airi'));}if(JP_CHOICES[raw])return JP_CHOICES[raw];if(JP_SPEAKERS[raw])return localizeSpeaker(raw);return raw.replaceAll('Airi',localizeNameToken('Airi')).replaceAll('Narration','地の文').replaceAll('System','システム');}

function playerName(){return (read('playerName','Airi')||'Airi').trim()||'Airi';}
function displayText(t){return localizeStoryText(t);}
function showNameSetup(){document.getElementById('playerNameInput').value=playerName();show('nameSetup');setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('playerNameInput').focus(),50);}
function setPlayerNameAndGo(){let v=document.getElementById('playerNameInput').value.trim();if(!v)v='Airi';store('playerName',v);show('routes');}
function kenjiUnlocked(){return read('kenjiUnlocked',false)===true || read('cgs',[]).includes('Kenji Sato Route Card / Portrait Reveal');}
function refreshMenuArt(){const wrap=document.getElementById('menuWrap'); if(wrap) wrap.style.backgroundImage = kenjiUnlocked()?"url('assets/menu_unlocked_kenji.webp')":"url('assets/menu_locked_secret.webp')";}
function kenjiMenuClick(){if(kenjiUnlocked())startRoute('kenji');else revealKenji();}
function revealKenji(){store('kenjiUnlocked',true);unlockCG('Kenji Sato Route Card / Portrait Reveal');renderRoutes();refreshMenuArt();show('kenjiReveal');}
function show(id){document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');if(id==='routes')renderRoutes();if(id==='saves')renderSaves();if(id==='menu')refreshMenuArt();applyLanguage();window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});}
function store(k,v){localStorage.setItem('tlg_'+k,JSON.stringify(v));}
function read(k,d){try{let v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tlg_'+k));return v??d}catch(e){return d}}
function unlockEnding(title,text,assets=[]){let arr=read('endings',[]);let existing=arr.find(x=>x.title===title);if(existing){existing.text=text;existing.assets=assets;existing.date=new Date().toLocaleString();}else{arr.push({title,text,assets,date:new Date().toLocaleString()});}store('endings',arr);}
function unlockCG(label){let arr=read('cgs',[]);if(label&&!arr.includes(label)){arr.push(label);store('cgs',arr);}}
function startRoute(r){route=r;if(r==='kenji'){store('kenjiUnlocked',true);unlockCG('Kenji Sato Route Card / Portrait Reveal');renderRoutes();refreshMenuArt();}stats={a:0,b:0,self:0,gravity:0,honesty:0};scene=DATA[r].start;document.getElementById('stat1name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA[r].meta[0]);document.getElementById('stat2name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA[r].meta[1]);show('game');render();}
function add(d){for(const k in d){stats[k]=Math.max(0,(stats[k]||0)+d[k]);}}
function go(next,d){add(d||{});if(next==='CHECK_TOMMY')return checkTommy();if(next==='CHOOSE_TOMMY')return chooseTommy();if(next==='CHOOSE_SELF')return end('Mini True Ending: No One This Time','Airi thought hearing Tommy say it would set her free. Instead, it only proved she was not crazy. The confession does not erase the damage. She chooses herself.');if(next.startsWith('END_'))return routeEnd(next.slice(4).toLowerCase());scene=next;render();}
function splitVNText(t){let raw=displayText(t||'');let chunks=raw.split(/\n\s*\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);return chunks.length?chunks:[raw];}
function resolveLinePresentation(text){let s=DATA[route]?.scenes[scene]||['','Narration','',[]];let speaker=s[1]||'Narration';let cleaned=String(text||'');const m=cleaned.match(/^(Camille|Tessa|Jade|Airi|Ren|Harrison|Marcus|Hiroshi|Kenji|Tommy|System|Narration|アイリ|蓮|ナレーション|システム)：?\s*([\s\S]*)$/);if(m){speaker=m[1];cleaned=m[2];}let artKey=null;if((s[0]||'').includes('Friend Council')){if(speaker==='Camille') artKey='friend_camille_phone';if(speaker==='Tessa') artKey='friend_tessa_phone';if(speaker==='Jade') artKey='friend_jade_phone';}return {speaker,text:cleaned,artKey};}
function startType(text){clearTimeout(typingTimer);const p=resolveLinePresentation(text);if(p.artKey) setStageArt(p.artKey, assetTitle(p.artKey));document.getElementById('speaker').textContent=displayText(p.speaker);typingDone=false;currentFullText=p.text;const el=document.getElementById('line');el.classList.add('typing');el.textContent='';let i=0;function tick(){if(i>=currentFullText.length){typingDone=true;el.classList.remove('typing');return;}el.textContent+=currentFullText[i++];typingTimer=setTimeout(tick,18);}tick();}
function completeTyping(){clearTimeout(typingTimer);document.getElementById('line').textContent=currentFullText;document.getElementById('line').classList.remove('typing');typingDone=true;}
function showChoices(){const s=DATA[route].scenes[scene];const box=document.getElementById('choices');box.innerHTML='';(s[3]||[]).forEach(c=>{let b=document.createElement('button');b.className='choice';b.textContent=displayText(c[0]);b.onclick=()=>go(c[1],c[2]);box.appendChild(b);});document.getElementById('advanceRow').style.display='none';}
function advanceText(){if(!route||!scene)return;if(!typingDone){completeTyping();return;}lineIndex++;if(lineIndex<lineParts.length){startType(lineParts[lineIndex]);}else{showChoices();}}
function assetTitle(k){return (ASSET_META[k]&&ASSET_META[k].title)||k;}
function setStageArt(key,fallbackLabel){const bgEl=document.getElementById('bg');const img=document.getElementById('stageImg');if(key&&CG_ART[key]){img.src=CG_ART[key];img.style.display='block';img.alt=assetTitle(key);document.getElementById('cg').textContent=displayText(assetTitle(key));}else{img.removeAttribute('src');img.style.display='none';img.alt='';document.getElementById('cg').textContent=displayText(fallbackLabel||'');}}
function render(){const s=DATA[route].scenes[scene];if(!s){alert('Missing scene: '+scene);return;}unlockCG(s[0]);const keys=SCENE_ASSETS[scene]||[];if(keys[0]) unlockCG(assetTitle(keys[0]));document.getElementById('stat1').textContent=stats.a;document.getElementById('stat2').textContent=stats.b;document.getElementById('self').textContent=stats.self;document.getElementById('gravity').textContent=stats.gravity;document.getElementById('honesty').textContent=stats.honesty;document.getElementById('bg').className='bg '+DATA[route].meta[2];setStageArt(keys[0]||'', localizeSceneTitle(scene,s[0]));document.getElementById('choices').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('advanceRow').style.display='flex';lineParts=splitVNText(s[2]);lineIndex=0;startType(lineParts[0]);}
function checkTommy(){
  if(route==='ren'){
    const enoughForConfession=(stats.self>=58 && stats.honesty>=42 && stats.gravity>=22 && (stats.tommyRisk||0)>=28);
    scene=enoughForConfession?'ren_tommy':'ren_tommy_dodge';
    return render();
  }
  if(route==='harrison'){
    const enoughForConfession=(stats.self>=62 && stats.honesty>=50 && stats.gravity>=28 && (stats.tommyRisk||0)>=30);
    scene=enoughForConfession?'harrison_tommy':'harrison_tommy_dodge';
    return render();
  }
  if(route==='marcus'){
    const enoughForConfession=(stats.self>=60 && stats.honesty>=48 && stats.gravity>=30 && (stats.tommyRisk||0)>=32);
    scene=enoughForConfession?'marcus_tommy':'marcus_tommy_dodge';
    return render();
  }
  routeEnd(route);
}
function chooseTommy(){
  const secretReady=(stats.self>=90 && stats.honesty>=72 && stats.gravity>=48 && (stats.tommyRisk||0)>=52);
  if(secretReady){
    if(route==='ren')return end('Secret Ending: Dream Wedding','Airi only reaches this impossible file by forcing every hidden feeling into the open and refusing to make Ren disappear quietly. Ren gets the truth, not a vanishing act. Tommy chooses her out loud, with no friendship label to hide behind.');
    if(route==='harrison')return end('Secret Ending: House in Hyogo','Airi tells the truth before the story can rot into secrecy. Harrison is not erased; he was real, and Tommy has to respect that. After the hard conversations, Airi and Tommy build a small life in Hyogo.');
    if(route==='marcus')return end('Secret Ending: Our Little Boy','Airi stops treating love like a disaster she has to survive. Tommy chooses her clearly, then keeps choosing her in the boring, daily ways that actually matter.');
  }
  return end('Tommy Variant: Back to You','Tommy finally says the words, but the pattern is not healed enough to become the impossible happy file. Airi chooses the maybe again, and the loop starts humming under the music.');
}
function routeEnd(r){
  if(r==='ren'){
    if(stats.gravity>=30)return end('Ren Bad Ending: The Door She Left Open','Ren chose Airi clearly, but Airi kept leaving emotional space open for Tommy. The relationship loses oxygen slowly.');
    if(stats.self>=35&&stats.a>=30&&stats.b>=25&&stats.honesty>=20)return end('Ren Good Ending: Chosen Every Day','Airi tells the truth, even when it makes her look messy. Ren does not ask her to be perfect; he asks her to be present. She chooses the man who does not make her guess.');
    return end('Ren Bittersweet Ending: Almost Over Him','Ren loved her and chose her, but Tommy’s emotional gravity kept haunting the relationship. Some truths arrived. Some arrived too late.');
  }
  if(r==='harrison_good')return end('Harrison Good Ending: Home Felt Like a Choice','Airi chooses Harrison, not because he is easy, but because peace is not the same thing as settling. The park, the museum, the red dress, the white party — none of it needed her to beg for clarity.');
  if(r==='harrison_job')return end('Harrison Bittersweet Ending: Loved Her Enough to Let Her Go','Airi chooses the opportunity and tells Harrison the truth before resentment can grow teeth. He loves her well enough not to cage her. It hurts because it was real.');
  if(r==='harrison'){
    if(stats.gravity>=30)return end('Harrison Bad Ending: Safe Was Not Enough','Harrison stayed, but Airi kept looking toward the door. Safety could not compete with a ghost she had not buried.');
    if(stats.self>=35&&stats.b>=28)return end('Harrison Good Ending: Home Felt Like a Choice','Airi chooses peace, honesty, and the man who kept showing up.');
    return end('Harrison Bittersweet Ending: The Right Man, Wrong Life','He made home feel possible, but Airi still had somewhere else to go.');
  }
  if(r==='marcus_break')return end('Marcus Bittersweet Ending: They Both Grew','Airi breaks up with Marcus before the relationship turns into a job. It hurts, but it is clean. Marcus grows because she stops cushioning every consequence for him.');
  if(r==='marcus_holding')return end('Marcus Bad Ending: Holding Him Down','Airi stays and tries to help Marcus hold everything together. Soon she is not his girlfriend so much as his emergency plan. The chemistry remains, but the respect drains out of the room.');
  if(r==='marcus'){
    if(stats.gravity>=30)return end('Marcus Bad Ending: Chemistry Is Not a Home','Airi keeps confusing intensity with safety. Marcus wants her, Tommy haunts her, and nobody gets steady ground.');
    if(stats.b>=28&&stats.self>=25)return end('Marcus Good Ending: He Got His Shit Together','Marcus grows up enough to love Airi without chaos being the whole language. It is not magic. It is accountability, time, and the choice to stop making her piece together the truth.');
    return end('Marcus Bittersweet Ending: Almost Enough Fire','The chemistry was real. The love mattered. Growth arrived too slowly.');
  }
  if(r==='hiroshi'){
    if(stats.b>=25&&stats.self>=18)return end('Hiroshi Good Ending: A Beautiful Temporary Thing','Airi enjoys the romance for what it is and leaves before resentment replaces affection.');
    if(stats.gravity>=20)return end('Hiroshi Neutral Ending: Your Prince?','Hiroshi is fun, but Tommy’s boyfriend-audit message makes Airi realize her heart is still crowded.');
    return end('Hiroshi Bad Ending: Stayed Too Long','Airi mistakes attention for attachment and stays past the expiration date. The fedora could not save this one.');
  }
  if(r==='kenji'){
    if(stats.gravity>=25||stats.self<25)return end('Kenji Bad Ending: Backup Plan','Kenji sees it before Airi can explain it. He loves her too much to become a bandage.');
    if(stats.a>=30&&stats.b>=30&&stats.self>=35)return end('Kenji Good Ending: Home Was Here Too','The right love did not chase her. It waited until she could recognize peace. Kenji was never Tommy’s friend. He was Airi’s — and that made all the difference.');
    return end('Kenji Bittersweet Ending: Too Precious to Risk','Some maybes are gentle. That does not make them painless.');
  }
  if(r==='tommy_friends')return end('Tommy Friends Ending: The Word Friend','Airi asked directly, and Tommy gave her the cleanest wound he knew: friend. It hurt because the word did not match the way he held her hand under the stars.');
  if(r==='tommy_friendship')return end('Tommy Friends Ending: Too Important To Risk','Airi confessed beneath festival lights, and Tommy hid behind the idea that loving her would ruin the friendship. The bear in her hands suddenly felt heavier than it should have.');
  if(r==='tommy_loop')return end('Tommy Bad Ending: Endless Loop','She lets the festival end without asking for a definition. The maybe survives. So does the ache. You have seen this scene before. You will see it again.');
  if(r==='tommy_true')return end('Tommy True Ending: The Long Goodbye','Love was real. The loop was killing her. So she left. Not because it meant nothing, but because almost was not enough to build a life on.');
  if(r==='tommy'){
    if(stats.self>=55)return end('Tommy True Ending: The Long Goodbye','Love was real. The loop was killing her. So she left.');
    if(stats.gravity>=55)return end('Tommy Bad Ending: Endless Loop','You have seen this scene before. You will see it again.');
    if(stats.b>=35&&stats.self>=30)return end('Tommy Heartbreak Ending: Almost Is Not Enough','She was not nothing. But she was not his girlfriend either.');
    return end('Tommy Ending: Forever Maybe','He stayed close enough for her to feel loved, and far enough to deny it.');
  }
}
function escapeHtml(v){return String(v||'').replace(/[&<>"]/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[ch]));}
function endingAssetsFor(title){return (END_ASSETS[title]||[]).filter(k=>CG_ART[k]);}
function renderEndingArt(keys){const wrap=document.getElementById('endingArt');wrap.innerHTML='';keys.forEach((k,i)=>{const frame=document.createElement('div');frame.className='ending-frame';frame.innerHTML=`<img src="${CG_ART[k]}" alt="${escapeHtml(assetTitle(k))}"><div class="caption">${i+1}. ${escapeHtml(assetTitle(k))}</div>`;wrap.appendChild(frame);});}
function renderEndingCopy(txt){return displayText(txt).split(/\n\s*\n/).map(p=>`<p>${escapeHtml(p)}</p>`).join('');}
function end(t,txt){const title=displayText(t);const body=displayText(txt);const assets=endingAssetsFor(t);assets.forEach(k=>unlockCG(assetTitle(k)));unlockEnding(title,body,assets);unlockCG(title+' CG');document.getElementById('endingTitle').textContent=title;renderEndingArt(assets);document.getElementById('endingText').innerHTML=renderEndingCopy(body);show('ending');}
function renderRoutes(){let g=document.getElementById('routeGrid');g.innerHTML='';Object.keys(routeNames).forEach(r=>{let d=document.createElement('div');d.className='card';const locked=(r==='kenji'&&!kenjiUnlocked());let title=locked?uiText('secretRoute','Secret Route ???'):(r==='kenji'?(isJapanese()?'Kenji Sato':'Kenji Sato'):routeDisplayName(r));let cover=locked?'<img class="route-card-img" src="assets/secret_route_locked_card.webp" alt="Secret route locked">':((ROUTE_COVER[r]&&CG_ART[ROUTE_COVER[r]])?'<img class="route-card-img" src="'+CG_ART[ROUTE_COVER[r]]+'" alt="'+title+'">':'');let btn=locked?'<button onclick="revealKenji()">'+uiText('revealRoute','Reveal Route')+'</button>':'<button onclick="startRoute(\''+r+'\')">'+uiText('play','Play')+'</button>';d.innerHTML=cover+'<h3>'+title+'</h3><p>'+routeTagDisplay(r)+'</p>'+btn;g.appendChild(d);});}
function renderSaves(){let saves=read('saves',Array(20).fill(null));let g=document.getElementById('saveGrid');g.innerHTML='';for(let i=0;i<20;i++){let s=saves[i];let d=document.createElement('div');d.className='slot '+(!s?'empty':'');let label=s?('<b>'+uiText('slot','Slot')+' '+(i+1)+'</b><p>'+routeDisplayName(s.route)+'<br>Player: '+(s.playerName||'Airi')+'<br>'+s.scene+'<br>'+s.date+'</p>'):'<b>'+uiText('slot','Slot')+' '+(i+1)+'</b><p>'+uiText('empty','Empty')+'</p>';d.innerHTML=label+'<button onclick="saveSlot('+i+')">'+uiText('save','Save')+'</button>'+(s?'<button onclick="loadSlot('+i+')">'+uiText('load','Load')+'</button><button class="secondary" onclick="clearSlot('+i+')">'+uiText('clear','Clear')+'</button>':'');g.appendChild(d);}}
function saveSlot(i){if(!route||!scene){alert('Start a route before saving.');return}let saves=read('saves',Array(20).fill(null));saves[i]={route,scene,stats:{...stats},playerName:playerName(),date:new Date().toLocaleString()};store('saves',saves);renderSaves();}
function loadSlot(i){let s=read('saves',Array(20).fill(null))[i];if(!s)return;route=s.route;scene=s.scene;stats=s.stats;if(s.playerName)store('playerName',s.playerName);document.getElementById('stat1name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA[route].meta[0]);document.getElementById('stat2name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA[route].meta[1]);show('game');render();}
function clearSlot(i){let saves=read('saves',Array(20).fill(null));saves[i]=null;store('saves',saves);renderSaves();}
function showEndings(){let arr=read('endings',[]);let g=document.getElementById('endingGrid');g.innerHTML=arr.length?'':'<p>'+uiText('noEndings','No endings unlocked yet.')+'</p>';arr.forEach(x=>{let keys=(x.assets&&x.assets.length?x.assets:(END_ASSETS[x.title]||[])).filter(k=>CG_ART[k]);let thumb=keys.length?`<img class="cg-thumb" src="${CG_ART[keys[0]]}" alt="${escapeHtml(x.title)}">`:'';let d=document.createElement('div');d.className='card';d.innerHTML=thumb+'<h3>'+escapeHtml(x.title)+'</h3><p>'+escapeHtml(x.text||'')+'</p><p class="small">'+escapeHtml(x.date||'')+'</p>';g.appendChild(d);});show('endings');}
function showCGs(){
  const owned=read('cgs',[]);
  const g=document.getElementById('cgGrid');
  g.innerHTML='';
  let rendered=new Set();
  GALLERY_ORDER.forEach(item=>{
    rendered.add(item.title);
    let unlocked=owned.includes(item.title)||owned.includes(item.key)||item.key.startsWith('route_')||item.key.includes('menu')||item.key==='secret_route_locked_card';
    let d=document.createElement('div');
    d.className='card cg-card';
    let visual=unlocked
      ? `<img class='cg-thumb' src='${CG_ART[item.key]}' alt='${escapeHtml(item.title)}'>`
      : `<div class='cg-thumb locked'>${uiText('lockedCG','LOCKED CG')}</div>`;
    d.innerHTML=`${visual}<h3>${displayText(item.title)}</h3><p>${item.route}</p><p class='small'>${unlocked?uiText('unlockedViewable','Unlocked and viewable in this build.'):uiText('lockedHint','Play the matching scene or ending to unlock this image.')}</p>`;
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
  owned.filter(x=>!rendered.has(x)).forEach(x=>{
    let d=document.createElement('div');
    d.className='card';
    d.innerHTML='<h3>'+displayText(x)+'</h3><p class="small">'+uiText('placeholder','Unlocked memory / placeholder CG.')+'</p>';
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
  if(!g.children.length){g.innerHTML='<p>No CGs unlocked yet.</p>';}
  show('cgs');
}
function playCuteClick(){try{const C=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!C)return;const ctx=window.__tlgAudioCtx||(window.__tlgAudioCtx=new C());const now=ctx.currentTime;const gain=ctx.createGain();gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,now);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.045,now+0.01);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,now+0.16);gain.connect(ctx.destination);[740,988].forEach((freq,i)=>{const osc=ctx.createOscillator();osc.type='sine';osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq,now+i*0.025);osc.connect(gain);osc.start(now+i*0.025);osc.stop(now+0.18+i*0.025);});}catch(e){}}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('button,.hotspot,.choice'))playCuteClick();});

(function applyV5610TommyHiddenAfterFestivalKissPatch(){
  const key = "tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss";
  const file = "assets/tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss.webp";
  const title = "Tommy Ultra Hidden CG · After the Festival";

  if (typeof CG_ART !== "undefined") {
    CG_ART[key] = file;
  }

  if (typeof ASSET_META !== "undefined") {
    ASSET_META[key] = {
      title,
      route: "Tommy Route",
      file: "tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss.webp"
    };
  }

  if (typeof GALLERY_ORDER !== "undefined" && Array.isArray(GALLERY_ORDER)) {
    const already = GALLERY_ORDER.some(item => item.key === key);
    if (!already) {
      GALLERY_ORDER.push({
        title,
        key,
        route: "Tommy Route"
      });
    }
  }

  if (typeof SCENE_ASSETS !== "undefined") {
    Object.assign(SCENE_ASSETS, {
      tommy_ultra_hidden_after_festival: [key],
      tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss_scene: [key]
    });
  }

  if (typeof END_ASSETS !== "undefined") {
    END_ASSETS["Tommy Ultra Hidden Ending: The Impossible File"] = [key];
  }

  if (typeof DATA !== "undefined" && DATA.tommy && DATA.tommy.scenes) {
    DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_ultra_hidden_after_festival = [
      "Tommy Ultra Hidden Ending · After the Festival",
      "Narration",
      "This is not the file that was supposed to exist.\n\nThis is the ending hidden behind every ending. The one that only opens after I survived the truth first.\n\nThe festival is still glowing somewhere behind us, but here, it is quiet.\n\nHe kisses me like he finally understands what almost cost me.\n\nAnd for one impossible moment, there is no maybe.",
      [
        ["Unlock the impossible file.", "END_TOMMY_ULTRA_HIDDEN", { gravity: 10, honesty: 10, self: 10 }]
      ]
    ];
  }

  // Wrap routeEnd so the new hidden ending resolves cleanly without rewriting the whole engine.
  if (typeof routeEnd === "function" && !window.__tlg_v5610_routeEndWrapped) {
    const previousRouteEnd = routeEnd;
    routeEnd = function(r){
      if (r === "tommy_ultra_hidden") {
        return end(
          "Tommy Ultra Hidden Ending: The Impossible File",
          "After every ending, after every maybe, after every version of goodbye, the hidden file opens."

      return previousRouteEnd(r);
    };
    window.__tlg_v5610_routeEndWrapped = true;
  }

  console.log("V5.6.13 Tommy hidden after-festival kiss CG patch applied.");
})();

/* =========================================================
   V5.6.13 NO MORE MAYBE CHEAT CODE PATCH
   Player-facing code entry + earned-message gate.
   Cheat code: NO MORE MAYBE
   ========================================================= */
(function applyV5612NoMoreMaybeCheatPatch(){
  const CHEAT_CODE = "NO MORE MAYBE";
  const CHEAT_FLAG = "tommyCheatEarned";
  const CHEAT_SEEN_FLAG = "tommyCheatMessageSeen";

  const REQUIRED_CORE_ENDINGS = [
    "Mini True Ending: No One This Time",
    "Ren Bad Ending: The Door She Left Open",
    "Ren Good Ending: Chosen Every Day",
    "Ren Bittersweet Ending: Almost Over Him",
    "Harrison Good Ending: Home Felt Like a Choice",
    "Harrison Bittersweet Ending: Loved Her Enough to Let Her Go",
    "Harrison Bad Ending: Safe Was Not Enough",
    "Harrison Bittersweet Ending: The Right Man, Wrong Life",
    "Marcus Bittersweet Ending: They Both Grew",
    "Marcus Bad Ending: Holding Him Down",
    "Marcus Bad Ending: Chemistry Is Not a Home",
    "Marcus Good Ending: He Got His Shit Together",
    "Marcus Bittersweet Ending: Almost Enough Fire",
    "Hiroshi Good Ending: A Beautiful Temporary Thing",
    "Hiroshi Neutral Ending: Your Prince?",
    "Hiroshi Bad Ending: Stayed Too Long",
    "Kenji Bad Ending: Backup Plan",
    "Kenji Good Ending: Home Was Here Too",
    "Kenji Bittersweet Ending: Too Precious to Risk",
    "Tommy Friends Ending: The Word Friend",
    "Tommy Friends Ending: Too Important To Risk",
    "Tommy Bad Ending: Endless Loop",
    "Tommy True Ending: The Long Goodbye",
    "Tommy Heartbreak Ending: Almost Is Not Enough",
    "Tommy Ending: Forever Maybe"
  ];

  function norm(v){return String(v||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[’']/g,"'").replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}
  function ownedEndingTitles(){return (read('endings',[])||[]).map(e=>norm(e.title));}
  function missingRequiredEndings(){const owned=ownedEndingTitles();return REQUIRED_CORE_ENDINGS.filter(t=>!owned.includes(norm(t)));}
  function earnedByEndings(){return missingRequiredEndings().length===0;}
  function setEarned(){store(CHEAT_FLAG,true);}
  function isEarned(){return read(CHEAT_FLAG,false)===true || earnedByEndings();}

  window.tommyCheatMissingEndings = missingRequiredEndings;
  window.tommyCheatEarned = isEarned;

  window.updateCheatBadge = function(){
    const badge=document.getElementById('cheatEarnedBadge');
    if(!badge)return;
    if(isEarned()){
      setEarned();
      badge.textContent='NO MORE MAYBE unlocked';
    }else{
      const left=missingRequiredEndings().length;
      badge.textContent=left ? left + ' core endings until code unlock' : '';
    }
  };

  window.checkTommyCheatEarned = function(announce){
    if(isEarned()){
      setEarned();
      updateCheatBadge();
      if(announce && read(CHEAT_SEEN_FLAG,false)!==true){
        store(CHEAT_SEEN_FLAG,true);
        setTimeout(()=>alert("A new file has appeared.\n\nYou have unlocked the code for the route that should not exist.\n\nCheat Code: NO MORE MAYBE"), 450);
      }
      return true;
    }
    updateCheatBadge();
    return false;
  };

  window.showCheatCodeScreen = function(){
    show('cheatCode');
    const input=document.getElementById('cheatCodeInput');
    const feedback=document.getElementById('cheatFeedback');
    if(input) input.value='';
    if(feedback){
      if(isEarned()){
        setEarned();
        feedback.textContent='A new file has appeared.\nEnter the earned code: NO MORE MAYBE';
      }else{
        const missing=missingRequiredEndings();
        const preview=missing.slice(0,6).map(x=>'• '+x).join('\n');
        feedback.textContent='Locked. Earn all core endings first.\n\nRemaining: '+missing.length+(missing.length?'\n'+preview+(missing.length>6?'\n• ...':''):'');
      }
    }
    setTimeout(()=>{if(input)input.focus();},50);
  };

  window.submitCheatCode = function(){
    const input=document.getElementById('cheatCodeInput');
    const feedback=document.getElementById('cheatFeedback');
    const entered=norm(input?input.value:'').toUpperCase();
    if(!isEarned()){
      const missing=missingRequiredEndings();
      if(feedback) feedback.textContent='Not yet. The game has not given you this code.\n\nRemaining core endings: '+missing.length;
      return;
    }
    if(entered!==CHEAT_CODE){
      if(feedback) feedback.textContent='That file does not open.\nHint: no more ____.';
      return;
    }
    store('tommyCheatEntered', true);
    unlockCG('Tommy Ultra Hidden Cheat Code: NO MORE MAYBE');
    startTommyUltraHiddenRoute();
  };

  window.startTommyUltraHiddenRoute = function(){
    route='tommy';
    stats={a:0,b:0,self:99,gravity:99,honesty:99,tommyRisk:99};
    scene='tommy_cheat_festival_confession';
    document.getElementById('stat1name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA.tommy.meta[0]);
    document.getElementById('stat2name').textContent=localizeStatLabel(DATA.tommy.meta[1]);
    show('game');
    render();
  };

  if(typeof SCENE_ASSETS !== 'undefined'){
    Object.assign(SCENE_ASSETS, {
      tommy_cheat_festival_confession: ['tommy_festival_solo_yukata_awake'],
      tommy_cheat_callout: ['tommy_festival_solo_stalls'],
      tommy_cheat_confession_unlocked: ['tommy_festival_solo_yukata_awake'],
      tommy_cheat_festival_kiss: ['tommy_festival_secret_kiss'],
      tommy_ultra_hidden_after_festival: ['tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss']
    });
  }

  if(typeof END_ASSETS !== 'undefined'){
    END_ASSETS['Tommy Ultra Hidden Ending: The Impossible File'] = ['tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss'];
  }

  if(typeof DATA !== 'undefined' && DATA.tommy && DATA.tommy.scenes){
    Object.assign(DATA.tommy.scenes, {
      tommy_cheat_festival_confession: [
        'Ultra Hidden File · NO MORE MAYBE',
        'Tommy',
        'The festival lanterns make everything look softer than it is.\n\nTommy walks beside me in his yukata, quiet in that familiar way that used to make me fill every silence for him.\n\nThen his fingers brush mine.\n\nNot by accident.\n\nHe takes my hand like he has already decided something, but his face still looks like he is trying to survive the decision.\n\n“It suits you,” he says.\n\nI look up at him.\n\n“The yukata, I mean.”\n\nHis ears turn pink.\n\n“Not that you don’t usually— never mind.”',
        [
          ['Ask him why he keeps doing this.', 'tommy_cheat_callout', {self:4,honesty:4,gravity:2}],
          ['Stay quiet and let the moment breathe.', 'tommy_cheat_callout', {gravity:4,honesty:2}]
        ]
      ],
      tommy_cheat_callout: [
        'Ultra Hidden File · What Are You Doing?',
        'Airi',
        'I lift our joined hands between us.\n\n“What are you doing?”\n\nTommy freezes.\n\nFor once, I do not rescue him from the silence.\n\n“You keep touching me like this. You keep looking at me like this. You keep making me feel crazy for noticing.”\n\nHis fingers tighten before he can stop them.\n\n“I don’t know.”\n\n“No.”\n\nMy voice shakes, but I do not let go.\n\n“I think you do.”',
        [
          ['Make him answer.', 'tommy_cheat_confession_unlocked', {self:6,honesty:6,gravity:4}],
          ['Back down.', 'END_TOMMY_LOOP', {gravity:5}]
        ]
      ],
      tommy_cheat_confession_unlocked: [
        'Ultra Hidden File · No More Maybe',
        'Tommy',
        'Tommy takes a breath like he is stepping off the edge of something.\n\n“I thought maybe you still had feelings for me.”\n\nMy chest goes tight.\n\n“Then why didn’t you say something?”\n\n“Because… if I cleared things up, I thought I might lose my friend.”\n\n“So you knew.”\n\n“I didn’t want to hurt you.”\n\n“You were hurting me the whole time.”\n\nHe looks down at our hands.\n\nFor one terrifying second, I think he is going to do what he always does. Smile. Deflect. Turn the whole thing into something safe enough to survive.\n\nInstead, he looks back at me.\n\n“I like you, Airi.”\n\nThe festival keeps glowing around us like the world did not just split open.\n\n“I think I always did.”',
        [
          ['Kiss him.', 'tommy_cheat_festival_kiss', {gravity:10,honesty:10,self:8}]
        ]
      ],
      tommy_cheat_festival_kiss: [
        'Ultra Hidden File · The Festival Kiss',
        'Narration',
        'I kiss him before he can take it back.\n\nOr maybe he kisses me first.\n\nYears of almosts collapse into one impossible moment under the festival lights.\n\nThis is not the ending the normal route allows.\n\nThis is the file that only opens after the game knows I survived the truth first.',
        [
          ['Leave the festival together.', 'tommy_ultra_hidden_after_festival', {gravity:10,honesty:10,self:10}]
        ]
      ],
      tommy_ultra_hidden_after_festival: [
        'Tommy Ultra Hidden Ending · After the Festival',
        'Narration',
        'This is not the file that was supposed to exist.\n\nThis is the ending hidden behind every ending.\n\nThe festival is still glowing somewhere behind us, but here, it is quiet.\n\nHe kisses me like he finally understands what almost cost me.\n\nAnd for one impossible moment, there is no maybe.',
        [
          ['Unlock the impossible file.', 'END_TOMMY_ULTRA_HIDDEN', {gravity:10,honesty:10,self:10}]
        ]
      ]
    });
  }

  // Wrap end() so the earned message appears immediately after the final required ending.
  if(typeof end === 'function' && !window.__tlg_v5612_endWrapped){
    const previousEnd=end;
    end=function(t,txt){
      const result=previousEnd(t,txt);
      checkTommyCheatEarned(true);
      return result;
    };
    window.__tlg_v5612_endWrapped=true;
  }

  // Wrap show() lightly so the main-menu badge updates.
  if(typeof show === 'function' && !window.__tlg_v5612_showWrapped){
    const previousShow=show;
    show=function(id){
      const result=previousShow(id);
      if(id==='menu') updateCheatBadge();
      return result;
    };
    window.__tlg_v5612_showWrapped=true;
  }

  // Enter key submits the code.
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const input=document.getElementById('cheatCodeInput');
    if(input && !input.__tlgCheatListener){
      input.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')submitCheatCode();});
      input.__tlgCheatListener=true;
    }
    updateCheatBadge();
  }, 200);

  console.log('V5.6.13 NO MORE MAYBE cheat-code patch applied.');

/* =========================================================
   V5.6.13 NO MORE MAYBE CHEAT ENTRY PATCH
   Adds a visible main-menu cheat-code option.
   Code: NO MORE MAYBE
   ========================================================= */

(function applyV5613NoMoreMaybeCheatEntryPatch(){
  const CHEAT_CODE = "NO MORE MAYBE";
  const CHEAT_UNLOCK_KEY = "tlg_no_more_maybe_cheat_unlocked";
  const CHEAT_EARNED_KEY = "tlg_no_more_maybe_cheat_earned";
  const ALL_ENDINGS_KEY = "tlg_all_endings_unlocked";

  function normalizeCode(value){
    return String(value || "")
      .trim()
      .toUpperCase()
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  }

  function markCheatEarned(){
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(CHEAT_EARNED_KEY, "true");
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  function hasEarnedCheat(){
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem(CHEAT_EARNED_KEY) === "true" ||
             localStorage.getItem(ALL_ENDINGS_KEY) === "true";
    } catch(e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function unlockCheat(){
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(CHEAT_UNLOCK_KEY, "true");
      localStorage.setItem(CHEAT_EARNED_KEY, "true");
    } catch(e) {}

    if (typeof state !== "undefined") {
      state.route = "tommy";
      state.scene = "tommy_cheat_festival_confession";
      state.stats = state.stats || {};
      state.stats.gravity = Math.max(state.stats.gravity || 0, 99);
      state.stats.honesty = Math.max(state.stats.honesty || 0, 99);
      state.stats.self = Math.max(state.stats.self || 0, 99);
    }

    if (typeof save === "function") {
      try { save(); } catch(e) {}
    }

    if (typeof DATA !== "undefined" && DATA.tommy && DATA.tommy.scenes && DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_cheat_festival_confession) {
      if (typeof render === "function") {
        render();
        return;
      }
    }

    alert("NO MORE MAYBE accepted. The hidden Tommy file is unlocked.");
  }

  function showCheatEntry(){
    const earned = hasEarnedCheat();
    const promptText = earned
      ? "Enter the code for the route that should not exist:"
      : "Enter cheat code:";

    const input = window.prompt(promptText, "");
    if (input === null) return;

    if (normalizeCode(input) === CHEAT_CODE) {
      unlockCheat();
    } else {
      alert("That code does not open this file.");
    }
  }

  function showEarnedMessageOnce(){
    const earned = hasEarnedCheat();
    if (!earned) return;

    try {
      if (localStorage.getItem("tlg_no_more_maybe_earned_message_seen") === "true") return;
      localStorage.setItem("tlg_no_more_maybe_earned_message_seen", "true");
    } catch(e) {}

    setTimeout(function(){
      alert(
        "A new file has appeared.\n\n" +
        "You have unlocked the code for the route that should not exist.\n\n" +
        "Cheat Code: NO MORE MAYBE"
      );
    }, 250);
  }

  function ensureCheatButton(){
    if (document.getElementById("noMoreMaybeCheatBtn")) return;

    const btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.id = "noMoreMaybeCheatBtn";
    btn.type = "button";
    btn.textContent = "Enter Cheat Code";
    btn.title = "Enter an earned route code.";
    btn.addEventListener("click", showCheatEntry);

    btn.style.position = "fixed";
    btn.style.right = "18px";
    btn.style.bottom = "18px";
    btn.style.zIndex = "9999";
    btn.style.padding = "10px 14px";
    btn.style.borderRadius = "999px";
    btn.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.35)";
    btn.style.background = "rgba(20, 18, 32, .86)";
    btn.style.color = "#fff";
    btn.style.fontWeight = "700";
    btn.style.letterSpacing = ".02em";
    btn.style.boxShadow = "0 12px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.35)";
    btn.style.backdropFilter = "blur(10px)";
    btn.style.cursor = "pointer";

    document.body.appendChild(btn);
  }

  // Add optional direct scene if the previous patch did not include it.
  if (typeof DATA !== "undefined" && DATA.tommy && DATA.tommy.scenes && !DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_cheat_festival_confession) {
    DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_cheat_festival_confession = [
      "Ultra Hidden File · NO MORE MAYBE",
      "Tommy",
      "The festival lanterns glow warm behind him.\n\nFor once, Tommy does not look away fast enough.\n\n“It suits you.”\n\nAiri blinks.\n\n“The yukata, I mean.”\n\nHis ears turn pink.\n\n“Not that you don’t usually— never mind.”",
      [
        ["Ask him why he keeps doing this.", "tommy_cheat_callout", { self: 2, honesty: 2 }],
        ["Let the moment breathe.", "tommy_cheat_callout", { gravity: 2 }]
      ]
    ];

    DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_cheat_callout = [
      "Ultra Hidden File · No More Maybe",
      "Airi",
      "“Tommy.”\n\nHe looks at me.\n\nI lift our joined hands between us.\n\n“What are you doing?”\n\nHis fingers tighten before he can stop them.\n\n“I don’t know.”\n\n“No. I think you do.”",
      [
        ["Make him answer.", "tommy_cheat_confession_unlocked", { self: 5, honesty: 5, gravity: 5 }]
      ]
    ];

    DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_cheat_confession_unlocked = [
      "Ultra Hidden File · The Words",
      "Tommy",
      "For once, he does not dodge.\n\nFor once, there is no joke, no maybe, no careful little escape hatch.\n\n“I like you.”\n\nMy breath catches.\n\n“I know I’m late. I know I was unfair. I know I kept acting like being close to you didn’t mean anything just because I was scared to name it.”\n\nHe looks at me like the words cost him everything.\n\n“But I like you, Airi.”",
      [
        ["Kiss him.", "tommy_cheat_after_festival_room", { gravity: 5, honesty: 5 }]
      ]
    ];

    DATA.tommy.scenes.tommy_cheat_after_festival_room = [
      "Ultra Hidden File · After the Festival",
      "Narration",
      "This is not the file that was supposed to exist.\n\nThis is the ending hidden behind every ending.\n\nHe kisses me like he finally understands what almost cost me.\n\nAnd for one impossible moment, there is no maybe.",
      [
        ["Unlock the impossible file.", "END_TOMMY_ULTRA_HIDDEN", { gravity: 10, honesty: 10, self: 10 }]
      ]
    ];

    if (typeof SCENE_ASSETS !== "undefined") {
      SCENE_ASSETS.tommy_cheat_after_festival_room = ["tommy_hidden_after_festival_kiss"];
    }
  }

  // Make the button visible whenever the UI has loaded. It works even before earning it,
  // so players who know the code can still enter it.
  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){
      ensureCheatButton();
      showEarnedMessageOnce();
    });
  } else {
    ensureCheatButton();
    showEarnedMessageOnce();
  }

  // Keep it available if route rendering replaces parts of the page.
  setInterval(ensureCheatButton, 1500);

  window.tlgEnterNoMoreMaybeCode = showCheatEntry;
  window.tlgUnlockNoMoreMaybeCheat = unlockCheat;

  console.log("V5.6.13 NO MORE MAYBE cheat-entry patch applied.");
})();

    kenjiRevealTitle:'Secret Route Unlocked', kenjiRevealBody:'His picture is now viewable. The secret card will stay revealed on the main menu and route select from now on.',
    kenjiRevealStart:'Start Kenji Route', backToRoutes:'Back to Routes',

  });
  Object.assign(JP_UI, {
    slot:'スロット', empty:'空き', save:'保存', load:'読込', clear:'削除',
    noCGs:'まだCGは解放されていません。', placeholder:'解放済みメモリー / 仮CG',

  function setText(sel, txt){ const el=document.querySelector(sel); if(el && txt!=null) el.textContent=txt; }
  function setHTML(sel, html){ const el=document.querySelector(sel); if(el && html!=null) el.innerHTML=html; }
  function setPlaceholder(sel, txt){ const el=document.querySelector(sel); if(el && txt!=null) el.placeholder=txt; }

  window.applyStaticLanguage = function(){
    const ja=isJapanese();
    // Name setup
    setText('#nameSetup h1', uiText('newGame'));
    setHTML('#nameSetup .panel > p:first-of-type', uiText('chooseName'));
    setText('#nameSetup .panel .small', uiText('chooseNameNote'));
    setPlaceholder('#playerNameInput', ja ? 'アイリ' : 'Airi');
    const nameBtns=document.querySelectorAll('#nameSetup button');
    if(nameBtns[0]) nameBtns[0].textContent=uiText('confirmName');
    if(nameBtns[1]) nameBtns[1].textContent=uiText('useCanon');
    if(nameBtns[2]) nameBtns[2].textContent=uiText('back');

    // Routes
    setText('#routes h1', uiText('routeScreenTitle'));
    setText('#routes .panel > p', uiText('routeScreenIntro'));
    setText('#routes .panel > button', ja ? 'メインメニューへ戻る' : 'Back to Main Menu');

    // Saves
    setText('#saves h1', uiText('saveLoadTitle'));
    setText('#saves .panel > p', uiText('saveLoadIntro'));
    setText('#saves .panel > button', uiText('back'));

    // Endings / CGs
    setText('#endings h1', uiText('endingsTitle'));
    setText('#endings .panel > button', uiText('back'));
    setText('#cgs h1', uiText('galleryTitle'));
    setText('#cgs .panel > p', uiText('cgIntro'));
    setText('#cgs .panel > button', uiText('back'));

    // About
    setText('#about h1', uiText('aboutTitle'));
    const aboutBtns=document.querySelectorAll('#about button');
    if(aboutBtns[0]) aboutBtns[0].textContent=uiText('aboutBtn');
    if(aboutBtns[1]) aboutBtns[1].textContent=uiText('back');

    // Cheat screen
    setText('#cheatCode h1', uiText('cheatTitle'));
    setText('#cheatIntro', uiText('cheatIntro'));
    setPlaceholder('#cheatCodeInput', uiText('cheatPlaceholder'));
    const cheatBtns=document.querySelectorAll('#cheatCode button');
    if(cheatBtns[0]) cheatBtns[0].textContent=uiText('submitCode');
    if(cheatBtns[1]) cheatBtns[1].textContent=uiText('back');
    const cheatLabel=document.querySelector('#cheatCode .panel > p:first-of-type');
    if(cheatLabel) cheatLabel.textContent = ja ? '超隠しファイル' : 'Ultra Hidden File';

    // Settings back button
    setText('#settingsBackBtn', ja ? 'メインメニューへ戻る' : 'Back to Main Menu');

    // Kenji reveal
    setText('#kenjiReveal h1', uiText('kenjiRevealTitle'));
    setHTML('#kenjiReveal .card p:nth-of-type(2)', uiText('kenjiRevealBody'));
    const kenjiBtns=document.querySelectorAll('#kenjiReveal .card button');
    if(kenjiBtns[0]) kenjiBtns[0].textContent=uiText('kenjiRevealStart');
    if(kenjiBtns[1]) kenjiBtns[1].textContent=uiText('backToRoutes');

    // In-game bottom panel
    setHTML('#game .panel.small', `<span class='small'>${uiText('tapAdvance')}</span><br><button onclick="show('saves');renderSaves()">${uiText('saveLoad')}</button><button class='secondary' onclick="show('routes')">${uiText('routeSelect')}</button><button class='secondary' onclick="show('menu')">${uiText('mainMenu')}</button>`);

    // Ending screen
    const endingPanel=document.querySelector('#ending .panel');
    if(endingPanel){
      const label=endingPanel.querySelector('p');
      if(label) label.textContent=uiText('endingUnlocked');
      const btns=endingPanel.querySelectorAll('button');
      if(btns[0]) btns[0].textContent=uiText('chooseAnother');
      if(btns[1]) btns[1].textContent=uiText('endingsTitle');
      if(btns[2]) btns[2].textContent=uiText('galleryTitle');
      if(btns[3]) btns[3].textContent=uiText('mainMenu');
    }

    // Menu labels that are visible text
    const menuCheatBtn=document.querySelector("#menu div[style*='bottom'] button");
    if(menuCheatBtn) menuCheatBtn.textContent = ja ? 'チートコード入力' : 'Enter Cheat Code';
  };

  const originalApplyLanguage = window.applyLanguage;
  window.applyLanguage = function(){
    originalApplyLanguage();
    applyStaticLanguage();
    updateCheatBadge();
  };

  window.updateCheatBadge = function(){
    const badge=document.getElementById('cheatEarnedBadge');
    if(!badge)return;
    if(isEarned()){
      setEarned();
      badge.textContent=isJapanese() ? 'NO MORE MAYBE 解放済み' : 'NO MORE MAYBE unlocked';
    }else{
      const left=missingRequiredEndings().length;
      badge.textContent=left ? (isJapanese() ? `コード解放まで残りコアED ${left}` : `${left} core endings until code unlock`) : '';
    }
  };

  window.showCheatCodeScreen = function(){
    show('cheatCode');
    const input=document.getElementById('cheatCodeInput');
    const feedback=document.getElementById('cheatFeedback');
    if(input) input.value='';
    if(feedback){
      if(isEarned()){
        setEarned();
        feedback.textContent = isJapanese()
          ? '新しいファイルが現れました。\n獲得したコードを入力してください：NO MORE MAYBE'
          : 'A new file has appeared.\nEnter the earned code: NO MORE MAYBE';
      }else{
        const missing=missingRequiredEndings();
        const preview=missing.slice(0,6).map(x=>'• '+x).join('\n');
        feedback.textContent = isJapanese()
          ? ('まだロック中です。先にコアルートのエンディングをすべて獲得してください。\n\n残り: '+missing.length+(missing.length?'\n'+preview+(missing.length>6?'\n• ...':''):''))
          : ('Locked. Earn all core endings first.\n\nRemaining: '+missing.length+(missing.length?'\n'+preview+(missing.length>6?'\n• ...':''):''));
      }
    }
    setTimeout(()=>{if(input)input.focus();},50);
  };

  window.submitCheatCode = function(){
    const input=document.getElementById('cheatCodeInput');
    const feedback=document.getElementById('cheatFeedback');
    const entered=norm(input?input.value:'').toUpperCase();
    if(!isEarned()){
      const missing=missingRequiredEndings();
      if(feedback) feedback.textContent = isJapanese()
        ? ('まだです。ゲーム側でこのコードが解放されていません。\n\n残りコアED: '+missing.length)
        : ('Not yet. The game has not given you this code.\n\nRemaining core endings: '+missing.length);
      return;
    }
    if(entered!==CHEAT_CODE){
      if(feedback) feedback.textContent = isJapanese() ? 'そのファイルは開きません。\nヒント：no more ____.' : 'That file does not open.\nHint: no more ____.';
      return;
    }
    store('tommyCheatEntered', true);
    unlockCG('Tommy Ultra Hidden Cheat Code: NO MORE MAYBE');
    startTommyUltraHiddenRoute();
  };

  // Ensure static text is correct immediately.
  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ()=>{ applyStaticLanguage(); updateCheatBadge(); });
  } else {
    applyStaticLanguage();
    updateCheatBadge();
  }

  console.log('V5.6.16 full JP script completion patch applied.');
})();

  window.JP_SCENE_TITLES_FULL = JP_SCENE_TITLES_FULL;
  window.JP_STORY_FULL = JP_STORY_FULL;
  window.JP_CHOICES_FULL = JP_CHOICES_FULL;

  const oldLocalizeSceneTitle = window.localizeSceneTitle;
  window.localizeSceneTitle = function(sceneKey, fallback){
    if(currentLanguage && currentLanguage()==='ja'){
      return JP_SCENE_TITLES_FULL[sceneKey] || (typeof JP_SCENE_TITLES!=='undefined' && JP_SCENE_TITLES[sceneKey]) || fallback;
    }
    return fallback;
  };

  const oldLocalizeStoryText = window.localizeStoryText;
  window.localizeStoryText = function(t){
    let raw=String(t||'');
    const lang=currentLanguage ? currentLanguage() : 'en';
    if(lang!=='ja') return raw.replaceAll('Airi', playerName ? playerName() : 'Airi');

    if(typeof scene!=='undefined' && scene && JP_STORY_FULL[scene] &&
       typeof DATA!=='undefined' && DATA[route] && DATA[route].scenes && DATA[route].scenes[scene] &&
       raw === ((DATA[route] && DATA[route].scenes[scene] && DATA[route].scenes[scene][2]) || '')){
      return JP_STORY_FULL[scene].replaceAll('アイリ', localizeNameToken ? localizeNameToken('Airi') : 'アイリ');
    }

    if(JP_CHOICES_FULL[raw]) return JP_CHOICES_FULL[raw];
    if(typeof JP_CHOICES!=='undefined' && JP_CHOICES[raw]) return JP_CHOICES[raw];
    if(typeof JP_SPEAKERS!=='undefined' && JP_SPEAKERS[raw]) return localizeSpeaker ? localizeSpeaker(raw) : JP_SPEAKERS[raw];

    return raw
      .replaceAll('Airi', localizeNameToken ? localizeNameToken('Airi') : 'アイリ')
      .replaceAll('Narration','地の文')
      .replaceAll('System','システム')
      .replaceAll('Ren','蓮')
      .replaceAll('Harrison','ハリソン')
      .replaceAll('Marcus','マーカス')
      .replaceAll('Hiroshi','ヒロシ')
      .replaceAll('Kenji','ケンジ')
      .replaceAll('Tommy','トミー');
  };

  // Polish the Japanese settings note now that full scene coverage exists.
  if(typeof JP_UI !== 'undefined'){
    JP_UI.settingsIntro = 'メニュー、選択肢、全ルートの日本語ローカライズを選べます。';
    JP_UI.settingsNote = 'V5.6.16では全シーンに日本語ローカライズ草稿を追加済みです。今後は口調や自然さをルートごとに磨けます。';
  }
  if(typeof EN_UI !== 'undefined'){
    EN_UI.settingsNote = 'Japanese mode now has coverage for every story scene. The JP text is a complete localization draft and can still be polished route by route.';
  }

  // Ensure language is re-applied immediately if the patch loads after render.
  try{ if(typeof applyLanguage==='function') applyLanguage(); }catch(e){ console.warn(e); }

  console.log('V5.6.16 full JP script completion pass applied: ' + Object.keys(JP_STORY_FULL).length + ' scenes, ' + Object.keys(JP_CHOICES_FULL).length + ' choices.');
})();

      const entered=norm(input?input.value:'').toUpperCase();
      if(entered!==CHEAT_CODE){
        if(feedback) feedback.textContent = isJapanese()
          ? 'そのファイルは開きません。\nヒント：NO MORE ____.'
